### SD2:0
Do Not Use

### SD2:1
Nothing seems to happen.

### SD2:2
After weeks of travel in the treacherous Khanark Rockies, you have finally reached the Sword Lands. These lush, coastal lands are notorious for being ruled by trading city-states, Merchant Lords, War Lords, and Rogue Lords of all kinds of creed.

### SD2:3
Add to that the fact that nearly all undesirables and criminals eventually end up fleeing to these parts, and also the fact that these lands are infested with humanoids, giants, and monsters of all sorts, and you begin to understand well why ...

### SD2:4
these lands are known as the Sword Lands!  Tired, you remind yourselves of the reason which prompted you to undertake such a long journey to these lands: to investigate, on behalf of the King of Bywater, who or what is causing the disruption of trade...

### SD2:6
(And remember: use the arrow keys  -not the mouse- to cross from one land level to another, even if you are in a boat!)

### SD2:7
A sign above the door of this establishment reads: "Rock Forest Waystation".  Do you enter?

### SD2:8
A sign above the door of this place reads: "The Laughing Tempest".  Do you enter?

### SD2:9
Unfurling a map which you purchased at great cost weeks ago, you find that you are near a tradeway town called Aranys, which is ruled by a Merchant Lord named Akkar Dhun.  A  stop there would help you catch some rest from your travels.

### SD2:10
Not feeling particularly charitable nor self-sacrificing, you turn and walk away, the sounds of battle fading with each step you take. Yet in your hearts, you do feel somewhat guilty about your decision ...

### SD2:11
Giant bees attack, seeing you as intruders!

### SD2:12
The bees defeated, you wonder where their hive could be ... perhaps you should set yourselves to find it some other day?

### SD2:13
A pack of boars attack, feeling threatened by your presence!

### SD2:14
You see from a distance a trading caravan under attack. Do you rush to help the caravan?

### SD2:15
Hungry giant beetles attack you!

### SD2:16
Angry wasps attack!

### SD2:17
Spiders attack, eyeing you as their meal!

### SD2:18
Hunting wolves, lead by their pack leader, have targeted you as their preys!

### SD2:19
A group of strange frogs attack you, obviously hungry!

### SD2:20
A band of thieves attack, wishing to relieve you of the burden of your valuables!

### SD2:21

### SD2:22
A pack of hungry rats move in for the kill!

### SD2:23

### SD2:28
A group of gnats make the jump on you!

### SD2:29

### SD2:30

### SD2:31
Strange worm-like creatures rise up from the ground and attack, mistaking you as lunch!

### SD2:32
You spot dangerous-looking creatures in the distance. Will you approach?

### SD2:33
Bearded, unkempt guards wave you impatiently through the gate. "Come on!", one yells, "Come on! Get through already, will ya? We don't have all day to wait on ya!" Obviously, security is lax in Aranys ...

### SD2:34
This appears to be Aranys' general store. Well stocked, but rather pricey. Do you wish to enter?

Enter

### SD2:38

### SD2:39
You bet!

### SD2:40
No way!

### SD2:41
After the battle, the Caravan Master comes up to you, all smiles. "Well fought, strangers! Those damn orcs are becoming bolder and bolder, I'd wager! If only we knew where they come from! In any case, please accept a token of our thanks!"

### SD2:42
After the battle, the Caravan Master comes to you, smiling. "Well fought! Those damn orcs are the bane of us, I say! I heard they lair somewhere north by the mountains. If only we knew where exactly! Anyways, please accept this token of our gratitude!"

### SD2:43
Exhausted after this needless confrontation, you realize that you could inherit some extra food supplies by processing further the carcasses of these animals, as a consolation.

### SD2:44
After this clash, you search further the fallen bodies. You find a map on a goblin who looked like the leader of this particular band.

### SD2:45
Some of the strange creatures dwelling in these woodlands attack you, perceiving you as a threat (or as preys? Or as meals?)!

### SD2:46
After the battle, the Caravan Master beckons you, smiling. "Well fought, strangers! It seems as if those damn marauder attacks keep increasing. In any case, you have our thanks indeed! Please accept this token of our gratitude!"

### SD2:47
After the battle, the Caravan Master beckons you, smiling. "Well fought! Those marauders are the bane of us, I say! Rumor has it that they are spawned from Dagger Keep, which would not be surprising. Anyways, please accept this token of our gratitude!"

### SD2:48
After the clash, you decide to search further the fallen bodies. You find more valuables!

### SD2:49
After the battle, the Caravan Master comes to you, smiling widely. "Well fought, strangers! Those goblins are sure becoming a nuisance of late! You have our thanks, indeed! Please, accept this token of our gratitude".

### SD2:50
The battle over, the Caravan Master beckons you. "Well fought, strangers! Damn goblin raids are increasing, but no one knows where they come from! You have our thanks! Please, accept this token of our gratitude".

### SD2:51
The battle over, the Caravan Master beckons you, smiling. "Well fought, strangers! Damn, those elusive goblins! I heard they possibly come from a secret lair in a forest west of here, near the mountains. Anyways, please accept this token of our thanks!"

### SD2:52
A group of marauders attack!

### SD2:53
Wisely, you decide to leave well enough alone; besides, the creatures do not seem to follow you in any case. Plenty more battles will soon be coming your way, you are quite sure of that!

### SD2:54
Hungry bears have chosen you for their next meal!

### SD2:55

### SD2:56
Green dragons swoop down on you for the kill!

### SD2:57
You spot a small group of green dragons, flying low and obviously on the hunt . Luckily, it appears that they haven't seen you. Do you attack the green dragons?

### SD2:58
Just as the dragons swoop down to catch whatever they were hunting, you jump in and attack!

### SD2:59
Lying very low, you watch as the dragons swoop down on whatever they were hunting. After their kills, you see them fly away with their catches. You notice that they are heading towards the nearby mountains.

### SD2:60
One of the dragons catches a glimpse of you as you were trying to hide away. He decides that you would make a better meal than whatever he and his friends were hunting: so the dragons swoop down and attack you instead!

### SD2:61
After the battle, the Caravan Master walks up to you, smiling widely. "Well fought, strangers! Well fought indeed! We owe you our thanks for your much needed help. Please accept this token of our gratitude!"

### SD2:62
After the battle, the Caravan Master beckons you, smiling. "Well fought, strangers! Those green dragons have been attacking caravans increasingly. We owe you our heartfelt thanks! Please, accept this token of our appreciation".

### SD2:63
"Have to go, now", the Caravan Master sighs while mounting his ride. "I bid you safe journeys, strangers!", he says, smiling again. Giving the sign to go ahead, he then slowly rides away, followed by the other escorts, and then the caravans themselves.

### SD2:64
The Caravan Master then shakes his head in frustration. "I tell ya", he says as he makes a fist to emphasize his point, "if only we knew where those damn dragons come from ... I'd be the first one to go there and put an end to their terror!".

### SD2:65
Strange, animated trees attack you!

### SD2:66
A group of badgers attack, perceiving you as a threat.

### SD2:67
Strange wolves attack you!

### SD2:68

### SD2:69
A group of adventurers, up to no good, decide to relieve you of your gold, items and other valuables!

### SD2:70
Some suspicious-looking people are approaching. Do you attack them before they do you?

### SD2:71
Initially surprised that you found them out, the bullies recover quickly and set out to do in the open what they were hoping to do quietly: do you in and take your valuables!

### SD2:72
You manage to move quickly enough (and without running) to elude your stalkers. They'll have to find other victims this time!

### SD2:73
After the battle, you search further your attackers. One of them has an old, crumpled map.

### SD2:74
From the smell of cooked food, raunchy alcohol, the blabbering noises, and the loud music, this place must be Aranys' inn.  And from the size of it, this inn must be the only one in town.  Do you enter?

### SD2:75
Pushing the door, you enter the inn.  A LOT of people of all sorts are here! Merchants, traders, mercenaries, adventurers, and most likely a cutpurse or two as well.  You look at each other, grinning: this is the place indeed!

### SD2:76
Do you want to leave?

### SD2:77
Pet silverback wolves are guarding this door. The Innkeeper must be quite wealthy and fortunate to have such rare pets! Do you back away from the wolves or do you confront them?

### SD2:78
Back away.

### SD2:79
Attack.

### SD2:80
Thanking the stars that no one has heard the battle, you gather yourselves and proceed onward.

### SD2:81
Bodyguards of the inn come to investigate the noise: upon seeing you, they attack!

### SD2:82
Now you've done it! This time, it is Zaran the Innkeeper himself, along with his consort Areyna, who have come to see what's all this fracas is about!

### SD2:83
Seething with anger at seeing intruders, Zaran and Areyna glare at each one of you.  "What's the meaning of this?", shouts Zaran.  Raising her sword, Areyna shouts in turn: "Explain yourselves!"

### SD2:84
Do you attempt at explaining your actions?

### SD2:85
At first surprised by your offer, Zaran then whispers to Areyna, who is still seething. After he finishes whispering, she begins to smile greedily.  You start fidgeting, wondering what the two of them have in store for you.

### SD2:86
"Your honesty and offer do have merit and interest", says Areyna in a steely voice.  Then Zaran chuckles.  "Aye! And it does take gall and guts indeed to sneak into our quarters in plain eye- and ear-shot of everyone else!", he exclaims.

### SD2:87
"You should do fine indeed!", he says, looking to Areyna. She then comes closer to you:"In reparation, we want you to find a magical item known as Nessar's Monocle. All we know is that the great thief Nessar set out to explore the Tlaran Caves long ago,

### SD2:88
and never came back.  His belongings, along with his Monocle, are presumed to lie somewhere deep in these caves.  You must go there and retrieve this item for us, agreed?"

### SD2:89
"Excellent!", exclaims Zaran. "Here is a map to the Tlaran Caves. We will wait until your safe and successful return". Areyna then raises a warning finger at you:"But until then", she adds sternly, "you will not be welcome here!". The bodyguards ...

### SD2:90
growl in agreement to that statement.  It looks like you will have to sleep under the moonlight for a while when you are in Aranys!

### SD2:91
You are then escorted out of the inn. "Remember", shouts Areyna before shutting the door, "You don't come back here until you find the Monocle!".

### SD2:92
Well, looks like you have quite a mission on your hand!

### SD2:93
Zaran frowns.  "Very well, then.  Instead, you will give us all of your money, in reparation for your intrusion".   Areyna nods firmly in agreement.  Do you comply?

### SD2:94
"All well and good", says Areyna as she accepts all your gold, gems and jewelry, "now, you will leave our inn!".  Zaran then puts up a hand to ward off Areyna's anger: "Now, now, dear. These people may come back any time they wish, provided ...",

### SD2:95
then he chuckles, "that they have the gold to pay.  After all, they did make reparations, and all accounts are square by me". Areyna then nods, reluctantly:"Agreed.  But tonight -", and then she points at each of you, "-you sleep under the moonlight!"

### SD2:96
You are then escorted out of the inn. "Remember", then whispers Zaran, as he is about to close the door, "come back anytime after tonight: I may yet have a use or two of your gall and guts!".   With a wink, he closes the door behind you.

### SD2:97
Zaran and Areyna, along with bodyguards, rush up to you.  "Well? Do you have it?", asks Zaran with great anticipation.  What do you answer?

### SD2:98
Both Zaran and Areyna are joyful. "Excellent! Give the Monocle and your debt for your troubles are fully repaid!", says Zaran.  Do you give the item away?

### SD2:99
Zaran happily snatches the Monocle from your hand and rushes to the private quarters.  Arenya looks at you thoughtfully: "I didn't think you would be as honorable and honest as you have been.  I thank you for that; take this gold for your troubles ..."

### SD2:100
Do you take the reward?

### SD2:101
Considering the circumstances, you thank Areyna for her generous reward.  Simply nodding, she adds: "Of course, you are now welcome here again.  But a warning: no more trouble out of you!".  Turning, she walks away to join Zaran.

### SD2:102
At your embarrassed refusal, Areyna smiles. "Truly, you are the principled and noble lot! I approve, and I am glad of it!  Take the reward and more, please, for it would make me happy."

### SD2:103
"The brass key is for the 3rd suite on your left, here at the inn, which is on the floor level", she adds, winking at you.  "Enjoy yourselves as long as you wish.  And thank you again for retrieving the Monocle".  She then walks away to join Zaran.

### SD2:104
"Well?", insists Zaran.  At your failure to produce the item, both he and Areyna go into a fit.  "This is the last time we brook insult from you, pathetic fools!", snarls Zaran. "Time to meet your Makers, and none too soon!", shouts Areyna.

### SD2:105
Calling their friends, allies, bodyguards and other mercenaries at their pay, Zaran and Areyna set themselves to teach you a final lesson.

### SD2:106
More friends, allies and hirelings join the fray!

### SD2:107
Still not believing your lucky stars to have survived such an ordeal, you make haste to get out.

### SD2:108
The door of the inn is boarded shut.  A sign hanging on the boards reads: "Closed until acquired by new owners".  You then notice some of the passersby glancing at you in recognition: they were probably there when you defeated Zaran and Areyna.

### SD2:109
Not wanting anymore trouble, you walk away from the closed inn, hoping no one will make a fuss about your presence in town.

### SD2:110
"So, you don't have it!", mumbles Zaran rather testily.  "Why bother us with such empty news?".   Areyna then chimes in: "Didn't we tell you not to come back unless to bring the item to us?".  You are then immediately ushered out by bodyguards.

### SD2:111
Areyna is seething.  "How-dare-you!  Not give us the item when you have it, will you?". "You owe us reparations",  yells Zaran as he unsheathes his sword, "and we will get it ... with your blood!"

### SD2:112
"Do you think us fools?", shouts Zaran. Areyna goes into a fit: "That's the last insult we brook from you, pathetic idiots!"

### SD2:113
Zaran, Areyna, and their bodyguards have at you, their weapons drawn.

### SD2:114
Then you stop, thinking that you should investigate the private quarters. After all the hassle you just went through, why not?  As you see that many people don't seem to make a fuss anymore about the battle, you're confident that you could do it!

### SD2:115
Zaran looks at you suspiciously. "Have you a room key? Show it to us!"

### SD2:116
Being unable to produce a valid room key, Zaran and Areyna go into a fit.  "This is the last insult we will brook from you, pathetic fools!", shouts Areyna.

### SD2:117
Zaran and Areyna look at each other, grinning, upon your showing of your room key.  "Well then!", exclaims Zaran, "All is understandable and forgotten.  However, perhaps you should go sleep off the alcohol in your room?".  Areyna nods in agreement.

### SD2:118

### SD2:119

### SD2:135

### SD2:136

### SD2:138
Zaran smiles.  "Excellent choice!"

### SD2:139
"Very well! Meals with drinks are 5 golds a piece; for all of you, that will be 30 golds."

### SD2:140
And almost immediately, large mugs of mead from the Dwarven Halls of the Khanark Rockies are served to you, with generous refills.  Excellent stuff all around!

### SD2:141
Almost immediately, large pints of cold lagger from the local brewery are served. Very good stuff indeed, and numerous refills as well!

### SD2:142
Almost immediately, large jugs of red wine imported from the great city of Tanirith are brought to you.  What a feast indeed!

### SD2:143

### SD2:144
Zaran chuckles. "Indeed we do, and a very classy one at that!  Ladies for men, and men for ladies!  Unfortunately, it is so popular, with so many patrons, than none are available!".  He bows in apology.  "Perhaps another time?"

### SD2:145
At that, you back away politely to make way for the other patrons waiting in line behind you.

### SD2:146
As you pass near a barbarian warrior (who is eating like a slobbering pig), you notice a set of brass keys lying next to his boots, just under the table.  Do you attempt at retrieving the keys?

### SD2:147
Without notice and with quickness, you bend down on all fours and retrieve the key.  The barbarian is oblivious to it all, being too much engrossed in his feasting and drinking.

### SD2:148
A key is dropped.

### SD2:149
A huge, burly bodyguard glares at you. "Have you a deluxe brass key?", he snarls, obviously suspicious that you do not have one.

### SD2:150
At your negative answer, he sniffs.  "I thought so!  This door leads to the inn's suites: only those with a heavy brass key may pass.  Now begone!".  He unceremoniously waves you away.

### SD2:151
As you show him your heavy brass key, the bodyguard's attitude changes drastically. "My apologies, sirs, myladies", he says as he makes a respectful bow.  He then opens the door for you.   "Have a good rest, sirs, myladies."

### SD2:152
The bodyguard bows as you pass through the door.  "Had a good rest, sirs, myladies?" he asks politely and nicely.  As you nod, he bows again, then resumes his watch.

### SD2:153
A bodyguard snarls at you.  "Have you brass keys?"

### SD2:154

### SD2:155
As you produce brass keys, the bodyguard bows.  "My apologies, sirs, myladies. Of course, you may go upstairs.  Enjoy yourselves".  He opens the door and lets you in.

### SD2:156
These stairs go up.  Do you wish to go upstairs?

### SD2:157

### SD2:158

### SD2:159

### SD2:161

### SD2:162

### SD2:163

### SD2:164
Obviously, this is a food storage room.

### SD2:165

### SD2:166

### SD2:167

### SD2:168

### SD2:169

### SD2:170

### SD2:171

### SD2:172

### SD2:173

### SD2:174

### SD2:175

### SD2:176

### SD2:177

### SD2:178

### SD2:179

### SD2:180

### SD2:181

### SD2:182

### SD2:183
At your approach, guards rush out of the compound and attack!

### SD2:184

### SD2:185
"Your transgressions are not to be tolerated!", he declares in a loud voice. "Leave now or face the consequences!"      Do you leave?

### SD2:186
You decide that this has gone too far, and leave the castle grounds.

### SD2:187

### SD2:188

### SD2:190

### SD2:191

### SD2:192

### SD2:193

### SD2:194

### SD2:195

### SD2:196

### SD2:198

### SD2:199

### SD2:200
You are in what appears to be Akkar Dhun's secret sanctum sanctorum.

### SD2:201
